// ------------------------------------------------------------------ secrets\n   177\t\n   178\t    private static Secrets readDbPassword(java.util.function.Function<String, String> env) throws ConfigException {\n   179\t        String inline = env.apply(ENV_DB_PASSWORD);\n   180\t        String file = env.apply(ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE);\n   181\t        if (inline != null && !inline.isEmpty() && file != null && !file.isEmpty()) {\n   182\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD + \" and \" + ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" are mutually exclusive\");\n   183\t        }\n   184\t        if (inline != null && !inline.isEmpty()) {\n   185\t            if (inline.length() < 16) {\n   186\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the database password must be at least 16 characters\");\n   187\t            }\n   188\t            return Secrets.ofString(inline);\n   189\t        }\n   190\t        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {\n   191\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD + \" or \" + ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" must be set\");\n   192\t        }\n   193\t        Path path = Path.of(file).toAbsolutePath().normalize();\n   194\t        if (!Files.isRegularFile(path, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   195\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" must name a regular file that is not a symlink\");\n   196\t        }\n   197\t        if (hasGroupOrOtherAccess(path)) {\n   198\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" must not be readable by group or other\");\n   199\t        }\n   200\t        byte[] raw = new byte[PASSWORD_MAX_BYTES + 1];\n   201\t        int read;\n   202\t        try (java.io.InputStream in = Files.newInputStream(path)) {\n   203\t            read = in.readNBytes(raw, 0, raw.length);\n   204\t        } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n   205\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" could not be read\");\n   206\t        }\n   207\t        if (read > PASSWORD_MAX_BYTES) {\n   208\t            java.util.Arrays.fill(raw, (byte) 0);\n   209\t            throw new ConfigException(ENV_DB_PASSWORD_FILE + \" is too large to be a password file\");\n   210\t        }\n   211\t        int lineEnd = indexOf(raw, read, (byte) '\\n');\n   212\t        int length = lineEnd < 0 ? read : lineEnd;\n   213\t        if (length > 0 && raw[length - 1] == '\\r') {\n   214\t            length--;\n   215\t        }\n   216\t        String value;\n   217\t        try {\n   218\t            value = new String(raw, 0, length, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n   219\t        } finally {\n   220\t            java.util.Arrays.fill(raw, (byte) 0);\n   221\t        }\n   222\t        if (value.length() < 16) {\n   223\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the database password must be at least 16 characters\");\n   224\t        }\n   225\t        return Secrets.ofString(value);\n   226\t    }\n   227\t\n   228\t    private static int indexOf(byte[] bytes, int length, byte target) {\n   229\t        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {\n   230\t            if (bytes[i] == target) {\n   231\t                return i;\n   232\t            }\n   233\t        }\n   234\t        return -1;\n   235\t    }\n   236\t\n   237\t    private static int boundedInt(java.util.function.Function<String, String> env, String name,\n   238\t        int fallback, int min, int max) throws ConfigException {\n   239\t        String value = env.apply(name);\n   240\t        if (value == null || value.isEmpty()) {\n   241\t            return fallback;\n   242\t        }\n   243\t        int parsed;\n   244\t        try {\n   245\t            parsed = Integer.parseInt(value.trim());\n   246\t        } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {\n   247\t            throw new ConfigException(name + \" must be an integer between \" + min + \" and \" + max);\n   248\t        }\n   249\t        if (parsed < min || parsed > max) {\n   250\t            throw new ConfigException(name + \" must be between \" + min + \" and \" + max\n   251\t                + \"; the minimum keeps the password hashing cost at an approved work factor\");\n   252\t        }\n   253\t        return parsed;\n   254\t    }\n   255\t\n   256\t    /**\n   257\t     * Reads {@code --data-dir=<path>} as a single argv element. A bare {@code --data-dir} is\n   258\t     * refused rather than consuming the next token, so an option can never swallow an argument\n   259\t     * that was meant for something else.\n   260\t     */\n   261\t    private static String firstOption(String[] argv, String flag) throws ConfigException {\n   262\t        String prefix = flag + \"=\";\n   263\t        String found = null;\n   264\t        for (String argument : argv) {\n   265\t            if (argument.equals(flag) || argument.startsWith(prefix)) {\n   266\t                if (found != null) {\n   267\t                    throw new ConfigException(flag + \" may be given only once\");\n   268\t                }\n   269\t                if (!argument.startsWith(prefix) || argument.length() == prefix.length()) {\n   270\t                    throw new ConfigException(flag + \" must be given as one argument, --data-dir=<path>\");\n   271\t                }\n   272\t                found = argument.substring(prefix.length());\n   273\t            }\n   274\t        }\n   275\t        return found;\n   276\t    }\n   277\t\n   278\t    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ directory guards\n   279\t\n   280\t    /** Creates (or accepts) a data directory that no other user can write into. */\n   281\t    static void ensurePrivateDirectory(Path dataDir) throws ConfigException {\n   282\t        rejectSymlinkComponents(dataDir);\n   283\t        if (Files.exists(dataDir, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   284\t            if (Files.isSymbolicLink(dataDir)) {\n   285\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory may not be a symbolic link\");\n   286\t            }\n   287\t            if (!Files.isDirectory(dataDir, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   288\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory must be a directory\");\n   289\t            }\n   290\t            if (isWorldWritable(dataDir)) {\n   291\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory may not be writable by other users\");\n   292\t            }\n   293\t            if (hasGroupOrOtherAccess(dataDir)) {\n   294\t                try {\n   295\t                    Files.setPosixFilePermissions(dataDir, OWNER_ONLY_DIR);\n   296\t                } catch (IOException cannotTighten) {\n   297\t                    throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory permissions could not be tightened to 0700\");\n   298\t                }\n   299\t            }\n   300\t        } else {\n   301\t            try {\n   302\t                Files.createDirectories(dataDir, PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(OWNER_ONLY_DIR));\n   303\t                Files.setPosixFilePermissions(dataDir, OWNER_ONLY_DIR);\n   304\t            } catch (IOException cannotCreate) {\n   305\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory could not be created\");\n   306\t            }\n   307\t        }\n   308\t        if (isWorldWritable(dataDir)) {\n   309\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the data directory is writable by other users\");\n   310\t        }\n   311\t    }\n   312\t\n   313\t    /**\n   314\t     * H2 creates a missing store file with the process umask, which is 0644 under this system's\n   315\t     * default, so the secret-bearing database would be world-readable. Pre-creating the file\n   316\t     * 0600 makes H2 write into it and preserve that mode (verified against H2 2.5.252); an\n   317\t     * existing file must already be 0600 or startup stops rather than quietly widening it.\n   318\t     */\n   319\t    static void ensurePrivateStoreFile(Path dbBase) throws ConfigException {\n   320\t        Path store = Paths.get(dbBase + \".mv.db\");\n   321\t        Path parent = store.getParent();\n   322\t        rejectSymlinkComponents(parent);\n   323\t        if (Files.exists(store, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   324\t            if (Files.isSymbolicLink(store)) {\n   325\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the account store may not be a symbolic link\");\n   326\t            }\n   327\t            if (!Files.isRegularFile(store, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   328\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the account store must be a regular file\");\n   329\t            }\n   330\t            Set<PosixFilePermission> actual = permissions(store);\n   331\t            if (!OWNER_ONLY.equals(actual)) {\n   332\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the account store must have owner-only permissions (0600), found \"\n   333\t                    + PosixFilePermissions.toString(actual));\n   334\t            }\n   335\t            return;\n   336\t        }\n   337\t        try {\n   338\t            Files.createFile(store, PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(OWNER_ONLY));\n   339\t            Files.setPosixFilePermissions(store, OWNER_ONLY);\n   340\t        } catch (IOException cannotCreate) {\n   341\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the account store could not be created\");\n   342\t        }\n   343\t        if (!OWNER_ONLY.equals(permissions(store))) {\n   344\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the account store could not be created with owner-only permissions\");\n   345\t        }\n   346\t    }\n   347\t\n   348\t    /** Bounds the audit log so a flood cannot fill the filesystem; truncation is logged. */\n   349\t    static void ensureAuditLogWithinBudget(Path auditLog) throws ConfigException {\n   350\t        if (!Files.exists(auditLog, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   351\t            return;\n   352\t        }\n   353\t        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(auditLog)) {\n   354\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the audit log may not be a symbolic link\");\n   355\t        }\n   356\t        if (!Files.isRegularFile(auditLog, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {\n   357\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the audit log must be a regular file\");\n   358\t        }\n   359\t        if (!OWNER_ONLY.equals(permissions(auditLog))) {\n   360\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the audit log must have owner-only permissions (0600)\");\n   361\t        }\n   362\t        // The size bound lives in AuditLog, so the startup bound and the append bound are one\n   363\t        // number, not two that can drift - and the retirement writes its marker inside the trail,\n   364\t        // not only on the terminal, so an erased history stays detectable from the file alone.\n   365\t        try {\n   366\t            new AuditLog(auditLog).retireOversizedTrail();\n   367\t        } catch (AuditLog.AuditException couldNotInspect) {\n   368\t            throw new ConfigException(\"the audit log could not be inspected\");\n   369\t        }\n   370\t    }\n   371\t\n   372\t    private static void rejectSymlinkComponents(Path path) throws ConfigException {\n   373\t        Path absolute = path.toAbsolutePath().normalize();\n   374\t        for (Path component = absolute; component != null && component.getNameCount() > 0;\n   375\t             component = component.getParent()) {\n   376\t            if (Files.isSymbolicLink(component)) {\n   377\t                throw new ConfigException(\"the data path may not contain a symbolic link: \"\n   378\t                    + component.getFileName());\n   379\t            }\n   380\t        }\n   381\t    }\n   382\t\n   383\t    private static Set<PosixFilePermission> permissions(Path path) throws ConfigException {\n   384\t        try {\n   385\t            return Files.getPosixFilePermissions(path);\n   386\t        } catch (IOException | UnsupportedOperationException unreadable) {\n   387\t            throw new ConfigException(\"permissions of \" + path.getFileName() + \" could not be determined\");\n   388\t        }\n   389\t    }\n   390\t\n   391\t    private static boolean isWorldWritable(Path path) throws ConfigException {\n   392\t        return permissions(path).contains(PosixFilePermission.OTHERS_WRITE);\n   393\t    }\n   394\t\n   395\t    private static boolean hasGroupOrOtherAccess(Path path) throws ConfigException {\n   396\t        Set<PosixFilePermission> actual = permissions(path);\n   397\t        return actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.GROUP_READ)\n   398\t            || actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.GROUP_WRITE)\n   399\t            || actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.GROUP_EXECUTE)\n   400\t            || actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.OTHERS_READ)\n   401\t            || actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.OTHERS_WRITE)\n   402\t            || actual.contains(PosixFilePermission.OTHERS_EXECUTE);\n   403\t    }\n   404\t\n   405\t    /** Marker so callers can distinguish a bad environment from a runtime failure. */\n   406\t    public static final class ConfigException extends Exception {\n   407\t        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n   408\t\n   409\t        ConfigException(String safeDescription) {\n   410\t            super(safeDescription);\n   411\t        }\n   412\t    }\n   413\t\n   414\t    /** A configuration that passed validation but whose store could not be opened. */\n   415\t    public static final class AppConfiguredException extends Exception {\n   416\t        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n   417\t\n   418\t        AppConfiguredException(String safeDescription, Throwable cause) {\n   419\t            super(safeDescription, cause);\n   420\t        }\n   421\t    }\n   422\t}\n===== CookiePolicy.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\t/**\n     4\t * Builds the {@code Set-Cookie} value for a session and refuses to emit one that would be\n     5\t * missing an attribute. There is no web server in this deliverable, so this is the component a\n     6\t * servlet filter or gateway would call; it exists so the cookie policy is code and testable\n     7\t * rather than a comment.\n     8\t *\n     9\t * <p>Every session cookie carries {@code Secure}, {@code HttpOnly}, {@code SameSite=Strict} and\n    10\t * an explicit {@code Path}, plus {@code Max-Age} and {@code Expires} derived from the server-side\n    11\t * expiry (never from a client-supplied value). {@code Secure} is unconditional: this program\n    12\t * never issues a cookie that would travel over cleartext, so an operator has no way to configure\n    13\t * the insecure variant by accident.\n    14\t *\n    15\t * <p>Names and values are validated against RFC 6265 rather than escaped, because a cookie whose\n    16\t * name or value needed escaping would mean the caller passed something it should not have.\n    17\t */\n    18\tpublic final class CookiePolicy {\n    19\t\n    20\t    /** Cookie name is fixed: an attacker-controlled name could collide with another application. */\n    21\t    public static final String SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = \"portal_session\";\n    22\t\n    23\t    /** Path is fixed by the operator's configuration, never by a request. */\n    24\t    private static final String ALLOWED_PATH = \"[A-Za-z0-9/_-]{1,128}\";\n    25\t\n    26\t    private CookiePolicy() {\n    27\t    }\n    28\t\n    29\t    public static String sessionCookie(String token, long expiresAtEpochSeconds, long nowEpochSeconds,\n    30\t        String path) {\n    31\t        if (!SessionStore.isWellFormedToken(token)) {\n    32\t            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"a session cookie requires a token issued by SessionStore\");\n    33\t        }\n    34\t        if (path == null || !path.matches(ALLOWED_PATH)) {\n    35\t            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"the cookie path is not acceptable\");\n    36\t        }\n    37\t        long remaining = expiresAtEpochSeconds - nowEpochSeconds;\n    38\t        if (remaining <= 0) {\n    39\t            remaining = 1;\n    40\t        }\n    41\t        if (remaining > SessionStore.SESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS) {\n    42\t            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"the session lifetime exceeds the configured maximum\");\n    43\t        }\n    44\t        StringBuilder cookie = new StringBuilder(220)\n    45\t            .append(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME).append('=').append(token)\n    46\t            .append(\"; Path=\").append(path)\n    47\t            .append(\"; Max-Age=\").append(remaining)\n    48\t            .append(\"; Expires=\").append(httpDate(expiresAtEpochSeconds))\n    49\t            .append(\"; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict\");\n    50\t        String rendered = cookie.toString();\n    51\t        if (!rendered.contains(\"HttpOnly\") || !rendered.contains(\"Secure\")\n    52\t            || !rendered.contains(\"SameSite=Strict\") || !rendered.contains(\"Path=\")) {\n    53\t            throw new IllegalStateException(\"the session cookie would be missing a required attribute\");\n    54\t        }\n    55\t        return rendered;\n    56\t    }\n    57\t\n    58\t    /** A cookie that deletes the session from the client. Attribute set is identical. */\n    59\t    public static String expiredSessionCookie(String path) {\n    60\t        if (path == null || !path.matches(ALLOWED_PATH)) {\n    61\t            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"the cookie path is not acceptable\");\n    62\t        }\n    63\t        return SESSION_COOKIE_NAME + \"=0; Path=\" + path\n    64\t            + \"; Max-Age=0; Expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT\"\n    65\t            + \"; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict\";\n    66\t    }\n    67\t\n    68\t    /** RFC 7231 IMF-fixdate; always GMT, computed from the server-side expiry. */\n    69\t    static String httpDate(long epochSeconds) {\n    70\t        return java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.RFC_1123_DATE_TIME\n    71\t            .withZone(java.time.ZoneOffset.UTC)\n    72\t            .format(java.time.Instant.ofEpochSecond(epochSeconds));\n    73\t    }\n    74\t}\n===== Db.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.sql.Connection;\n     4\timport java.sql.SQLException;\n     5\timport java.sql.Statement;\n     6\t\n     7\t/**\n     8\t * Owns the single JDBC connection and the schema.\n     9\t *\n    10\t * <p>Autocommit is off for the lifetime of the connection. Every check-then-act path runs\n    11\t * inside {@link #inTransaction}, and the throttle additionally takes a row lock, so \"count,\n    12\t * then allow\" and \"verify, then grant\" are single atomic statements or single locked\n    13\t * transactions. A transaction that throws rolls back completely: no partially created account,\n    14\t * no half-consumed throttle budget.\n    15\t */\n    16\tpublic final class Db implements AutoCloseable {\n    17\t\n    18\t    /** Fail closed rather than queue forever when another writer holds a throttle row. */\n    19\t    static final int LOCK_TIMEOUT_MILLIS = 2_000;\n    20\t\n    21\t    private final Connection connection;\n    22\t    private boolean retired;\n    23\t\n    24\t    Db(Connection connection) {\n    25\t        this.connection = connection;\n    26\t        try {\n    27\t            connection.setAutoCommit(false);\n    28\t            try (Statement lockSetup = connection.createStatement()) {\n    29\t                lockSetup.execute(\"SET LOCK_TIMEOUT \" + LOCK_TIMEOUT_MILLIS);\n    30\t            }\n    31\t        } catch (SQLException couldNotConfigure) {\n    32\t            throw new IllegalStateException(\"the account store could not be configured\", couldNotConfigure);\n    33\t        }\n    34\t    }\n    35\t\n    36\t    public Connection connection() {\n    37\t        return retired ? null : connection;\n    38\t    }\n    39\t\n    40\t    /**\n    41\t     * The account table, with the credential column sized from the longest hash the key-derivation\n    42\t     * policy can emit ({@link PasswordHasher#MAX_STORED_HASH_CHARS}) rather than from the width of\n    43\t     * today's defaults. A fixed 97 fitted the shipped parameters exactly and rejected a legal\n    44\t     * stronger one - a configured cost must never be refused by storage. The seven {@code %d}\n    45\t     * placeholders are the only format specifiers; the literal percent signs in the LIKE pattern\n    46\t     * and in the e-mail character class are written {@code %%}.\n    47\t     */\n    48\t    static final String ACCOUNTS_DDL = (\"\"\"\n    49\t        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS accounts (\n    50\t          id                  BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,\n    51\t          email               VARCHAR(254) NOT NULL,\n    52\t          username            VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL,\n    53\t          password_hash       VARCHAR(%d)  NOT NULL,\n    54\t          kdf                 VARCHAR(16)  NOT NULL,\n    55\t          kdf_memory_kib      INT          NOT NULL,\n    56\t          kdf_iterations      INT          NOT NULL,\n    57\t          kdf_parallelism     INT          NOT NULL,\n    58\t          created_at          BIGINT       NOT NULL,\n    59\t          password_updated_at BIGINT       NOT NULL,\n    60\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_email_unique UNIQUE (email),\n    61\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_username_unique UNIQUE (username),\n    62\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_kdf CHECK (kdf = 'argon2id'),\n    63\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_kdf_memory CHECK (kdf_memory_kib BETWEEN %d AND %d),\n    64\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_kdf_iterations CHECK (kdf_iterations BETWEEN %d AND %d),\n    65\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_kdf_parallelism CHECK (kdf_parallelism BETWEEN %d AND %d),\n    66\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_hash_matches_kdf CHECK (password_hash LIKE\n    67\t              '$argon2id$v=19$m=' || CAST(kdf_memory_kib AS VARCHAR)\n    68\t              || ',t=' || CAST(kdf_iterations AS VARCHAR)\n    69\t              || ',p=' || CAST(kdf_parallelism AS VARCHAR) || '$%%'),\n    70\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_email_lower CHECK (email = LOWER(email) AND ASCII(email) <> 32),\n    71\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_email_shape CHECK (email REGEXP\n    72\t              '^[A-Za-z0-9._%%+\\\\-]{1,64}@[A-Za-z0-9.\\\\-]{1,253}\\\\z'\n    73\t              AND INSTR(email, '..') = 0\n    74\t              AND SUBSTR(email, 1, 1) <> '.'\n    75\t              AND INSTR(SUBSTR(email, INSTR(email, '@') + 1), '.') > 0),\n    76\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_username_shape CHECK (username REGEXP\n    77\t              '^[a-z][a-z0-9\\\\-]{1,30}[a-z0-9]\\\\z'),\n    78\t          CONSTRAINT accounts_hash_shape CHECK (password_hash REGEXP\n    79\t              '^\\\\$argon2id\\\\$v=19\\\\$m=[0-9]{1,9},t=[0-9]{1,4},p=[0-9]{1,3}\\\\$[A-Za-z0-9+/]{22}\\\\$[A-Za-z0-9+/]{43}\\\\z')\n    80\t        )\"\"\").formatted(PasswordHasher.MAX_STORED_HASH_CHARS,\n    81\t            PasswordHasher.STORED_MIN_MEMORY_KIB, PasswordHasher.STORED_MAX_MEMORY_KIB,\n    82\t            PasswordHasher.STORED_MIN_ITERATIONS, PasswordHasher.STORED_MAX_ITERATIONS,\n    83\t            PasswordHasher.STORED_MIN_PARALLELISM, PasswordHasher.STORED_MAX_PARALLELISM);\n    84\t\n    85\t    /**\n    86\t     * Creates the schema if absent. Called once at start-up, before any credential is written.\n    87\t     *\n    88\t     * <p>The CHECK constraints repeat, in SQL, the character-class and format rules that\n    89\t     * {@link InputPolicy} enforces in Java - defence in depth, so a future code path that\n    90\t     * forgets to validate still cannot persist an email containing a newline, a username with\n    91\t     * markup, or the same mailbox spelled two different ways. Verified against H2 2.5.252:\n    92\t     * {@code REGEXP} is usable inside a CHECK constraint, and Java's {@code $} anchor is not\n    93\t     * acceptable here because Java lets a trailing newline match - SQL {@code \\z} is used, which\n    94\t     * refuses {@code \"user@corp.example\\n\"}.\n    95\t     *\n    96\t     * <p>The width and the cost CHECK constraints come from {@link PasswordHasher}, so the storage\n    97\t     * limit and the accepted-parameter policy are one fact in one place; the exact-shape regex\n    98\t     * still makes an over-long or padded value a size error rather than a silent truncation. The\n    99\t     * {@code accounts_hash_matches_kdf} constraint ties the cost parameters embedded in the hash\n   100\t     * string to their indexed columns, so the two can never disagree and an operator can find\n   101\t     * every account that needs re-hashing without parsing strings.\n   102\t     */\n   103\t    public void applySchema() throws SQLException {\n   104\t        try (Statement schema = connection.createStatement()) {\n   105\t            schema.execute(ACCOUNTS_DDL);\n   106\t\n   107\t            schema.execute(\"\"\"\n   108\t                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_guard (\n   109\t                  account_id     BIGINT       NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n   110\t                  failures       INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n   111\t                  window_start   BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n   112\t                  window_seconds INT          NOT NULL,\n   113\t                  max_failures   INT          NOT NULL,\n   114\t                  blocked_until  BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n   115\t                  version        BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n   116\t                  CONSTRAINT auth_guard_window CHECK (window_seconds BETWEEN 10 AND 86400),\n   117\t                  CONSTRAINT auth_guard_max CHECK (max_failures BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),\n   118\t                  CONSTRAINT auth_guard_failures CHECK (failures BETWEEN 0 AND 1000)\n   119\t                )\"\"\");\n   120\t\n   121\t            schema.execute(\"\"\"\n   122\t                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS throttle_bucket (\n   123\t                  bucket_key     VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n   124\t                  tokens         INT          NOT NULL,\n   125\t                  window_start   BIGINT       NOT NULL,\n   126\t                  window_seconds INT          NOT NULL,\n   127\t                  max_tokens     INT          NOT NULL,\n   128\t                  CONSTRAINT throttle_window CHECK (window_seconds BETWEEN 10 AND 86400),\n   129\t                  CONSTRAINT throttle_max CHECK (max_tokens BETWEEN 1 AND 100000),\n   130\t                  CONSTRAINT throttle_tokens CHECK (tokens BETWEEN 0 AND 100000)\n   131\t                )\"\"\");\n   132\t\n   133\t            schema.execute(\"\"\"\n   134\t                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (\n   135\t                  token_hash   CHAR(64)  NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n   136\t                  account_id   BIGINT    NOT NULL,\n   137\t                  issued_at    BIGINT    NOT NULL,\n   138\t                  expires_at   BIGINT    NOT NULL,\n   139\t                  revoked_at   BIGINT,\n   140\t                  CONSTRAINT sessions_token_shape CHECK (token_hash REGEXP '^[0-9a-f]{64}\\\\z'),\n   141\t                  CONSTRAINT sessions_expiry CHECK (expires_at > issued_at),\n   142\t                  CONSTRAINT sessions_account_fk FOREIGN KEY (account_id)\n   143\t                      REFERENCES accounts (id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n   144\t                )\"\"\");\n   145\t            schema.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sessions_account_idx ON sessions (account_id)\");\n   146\t        }\n   147\t        connection.commit();\n   148\t    }\n   149\t\n   150\t    /**\n   151\t     * Runs {@code body} in one transaction; commits on success, rolls back on any failure.\n   152\t     *\n   153\t     * <p>The monitor serialises access: the JDBC connection is confined to one thread at a time\n   154\t     * and two callers can never interleave work on it. A nested call reuses the open transaction\n   155\t     * and does not commit early.\n   156\t     */\n   157\t    public synchronized <T> T inTransaction(SqlBody<T> body) throws SQLException, AuditLog.AuditException {\n   158\t        if (retired) {\n   159\t            throw new SQLException(\"the account store is closed\");\n   160\t        }\n   161\t        if (depth > 0) {\n   162\t            depth++;\n   163\t            try {\n   164\t                return body.execute(connection);\n   165\t            } finally {\n   166\t                depth--;\n   167\t            }\n   168\t        }\n   169\t        depth = 1;\n   170\t        try {\n   171\t            T result = body.execute(connection);\n   172\t            connection.commit();\n   173\t            return result;\n   174\t        } catch (SQLException | AuditLog.AuditException | RuntimeException failed) {\n   175\t            try {\n   176\t                connection.rollback();\n   177\t            } catch (SQLException rollbackFailed) {\n   178\t                failed.addSuppressed(rollbackFailed);\n   179\t            }\n   180\t            throw failed;\n   181\t        } finally {\n   182\t            depth = 0;\n   183\t        }\n   184\t    }\n   185\t\n   186\t    private int depth;\n   187\t\n   188\t    public interface SqlBody<T> {\n   189\t        /**\n   190\t         * A body may fail with {@link AuditLog.AuditException} to abort the transaction: a\n   191\t         * security decision whose audit record could not be written must not be committed, so the\n   192\t         * rollback is the fail-closed behaviour rather than a partial one.\n   193\t         */\n   194\t        T execute(Connection connection) throws SQLException, AuditLog.AuditException;\n   195\t    }\n   196\t\n   197\t    public void close() {\n   198\t        if (retired) {\n   199\t            return;\n   200\t        }\n   201\t        retired = true;\n   202\t        try {\n   203\t            connection.rollback();\n   204\t        } catch (SQLException ignore) {\n   205\t            // Nothing useful can be done while shutting down; the store is closed below.\n   206\t        }\n   207\t        try {\n   208\t            connection.close();\n   209\t        } catch (SQLException ignore) {\n   210\t            // Same as above.\n   211\t        }\n   212\t    }\n   213\t}\n===== Digests.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.security.MessageDigest;\n     4\timport java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;\n     5\t\n     6\t/** Hashing helpers for non-secret inputs and for keyed lookups of high-entropy values. */\n     7\tpublic final class Digests {\n     8\t\n     9\t    private Digests() {\n    10\t    }\n    11\t\n    12\t    public static byte[] sha256(byte[] input) {\n    13\t        try {\n    14\t            return MessageDigest.getInstance(\"SHA-256\").digest(input);\n    15\t        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException impossible) {\n    16\t            throw new IllegalStateException(\"SHA-256 is mandated by the JCA specification\", impossible);\n    17\t        }\n    18\t    }\n    19\t\n    20\t    public static String sha256Hex(byte[] input) {\n    21\t        return hex(sha256(input));\n    22\t    }\n    23\t\n    24\t    /** Lower-case hex; used for DB primary keys and for non-reversible log tags. */\n    25\t    public static String hex(byte[] bytes) {\n    26\t        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(bytes.length * 2);\n    27\t        for (byte b : bytes) {\n    28\t            out.append(Character.forDigit((b >> 4) & 0xF, 16)).append(Character.forDigit(b & 0xF, 16));\n    29\t        }\n    30\t        return out.toString();\n    31\t    }\n    32\t}\n===== Gate.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\t/**\n     4\t * Wraps {@link AuditLog} so that a security decision cannot be returned before its record was\n     5\t * written. Every method throws {@link AuditLog.AuditException} on a write failure, and callers\n     6\t * translate that into a refusal - the trail cannot be silently lost by an attacker who fills the\n     7\t * disk, replaces the log with a directory, or widens its permissions.\n     8\t *\n     9\t * <p>Field values passed through here are always code-supplied constants or non-reversible\n    10\t * digests; see {@link AuditLog}.\n    11\t */\n    12\tpublic final class Gate {\n    13\t\n    14\t    /** Per-process salt so correlation tags do not survive as stable row identifiers. */\n    15\t    private final String processSalt = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();\n    16\t\n    17\t    private final AuditLog audit;\n    18\t\n    19\t    public Gate(AuditLog audit) {\n    20\t        this.audit = audit;\n    21\t    }\n    22\t\n    23\t    /** Records the event or throws; there is no variant that swallows a failure. */\n    24\t    public void record(AuditLog.Event event, String... fields) throws AuditLog.AuditException {\n    25\t        audit.record(event, fields);\n    26\t    }\n    27\t\n    28\t    /**\n    29\t     * Non-reversible correlation tag for an account: the primary key hashed with a per-process\n    30\t     * random salt, so records can be joined without writing a row identifier that maps directly\n    31\t     * onto the database.\n    32\t     */\n    33\t    public String accountTag(long accountId) {\n    34\t        return tag(\"account\", Long.toString(accountId));\n    35\t    }\n    36\t\n    37\t    /** Non-reversible tag for a source identifier. */\n    38\t    public String sourceTag(String sourceId) {\n    39\t        if (sourceId == null) {\n    40\t            return \"unknown\";\n    41\t        }\n    42\t        return tag(\"src\", sourceId);\n    43\t    }\n    44\t\n    45\t    /**\n    46\t     * Tags for identifying data. Because a username or an email address is low-entropy, these are\n    47\t     * salted per process: records can be joined within one run of the program, but the trail cannot\n    48\t     * be joined back to database rows, and two runs of the program produce unrelated tags. See the\n    49\t     * README for exactly what that does and does not protect.\n    50\t     */\n    51\t    public String emailTag(String canonicalEmail) {\n    52\t        return tag(\"email\", canonicalEmail);\n    53\t    }\n    54\t\n    55\t    public String usernameTag(String username) {\n    56\t        return tag(\"username\", username);\n    57\t    }\n    58\t\n    59\t    /**\n    60\t     * Tag for a session token. A token is a bearer credential, so neither the token nor its\n    61\t     * unstored SHA-256 may appear in the trail: a trail reader could otherwise sign in with\n    62\t     * whatever they can read. This digest is additionally salted per process, so it cannot be\n    63\t     * matched against the {@code sessions} table either.\n    64\t     */\n    65\t    public String sessionTag(String token) {\n    66\t        if (token == null) {\n    67\t            return \"absent\";\n    68\t        }\n    69\t        return tag(\"session\", token);\n    70\t    }\n    71\t\n    72\t    private String tag(String kind, String value) {\n    73\t        return Digests.sha256Hex((processSalt + ':' + kind + ':' + value)\n    74\t            .getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)).substring(0, 16);\n    75\t    }\n    76\t\n    77\t    public AuditLog log() {\n    78\t        return audit;\n    79\t    }\n    80\t}\n===== InputPolicy.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.util.Set;\n     4\t\n     5\t/**\n     6\t * The single trust boundary for every externally supplied value.\n     7\t *\n     8\t * <p>Nothing is normalised into a different identity: inputs are either accepted exactly as\n     9\t * supplied (email is case-folded, which is the documented mailbox-equivalence rule for the\n    10\t * domain and is applied consistently on both paths) or refused. There is no\n    11\t * {@code trim()}/{@code strip()} step anywhere, because stripping turns\n    12\t * {@code \"user@corp.example \"} or a trailing tab into the real account and undoes every\n    13\t * downstream check.\n    14\t *\n    15\t * <p>Reason strings returned in {@link ValidationException} describe the violated rule only.\n    16\t * They never contain the offending value, so they can be written to a log safely.\n    17\t */\n    18\tpublic final class InputPolicy {\n    19\t\n    20\t    /** Hard ceiling on any single field, applied before decoding so an oversized line cannot be buffered. */\n    21\t    public static final int MAX_FIELD_CHARS = 4096;\n    22\t\n    23\t    public static final int EMAIL_MAX = 254;\n    24\t    public static final int EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX = 64;\n    25\t    public static final int EMAIL_LABEL_MAX = 63;\n    26\t\n    27\t    public static final int USERNAME_MIN = 3;\n    28\t    public static final int USERNAME_MAX = 32;\n    29\t\n    30\t    public static final int PASSWORD_MIN = 12;\n    31\t    public static final int PASSWORD_MAX = 256;\n    32\t\n    33\t    private static final Set<String> RESERVED_USERNAMES = Set.of(\n    34\t        \"admin\", \"administrator\", \"root\", \"support\", \"helpdesk\", \"postmaster\",\n    35\t        \"webmaster\", \"abuse\", \"security\", \"noreply\", \"no-reply\", \"system\", \"sysadmin\");\n    36\t\n    37\t    private InputPolicy() {\n    38\t    }\n    39\t\n    40\t    /** A violated rule. The message is safe to log: it never contains user input. */\n    41\t    public static final class ValidationException extends Exception {\n    42\t        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n    43\t\n    44\t        ValidationException(String rule) {\n    45\t            super(rule);\n    46\t        }\n    47\t    }\n    48\t\n    49\t    /**\n    50\t     * @return the canonical form (email lower-cased) to store and compare\n    51\t     * @throws ValidationException if the value is not acceptable exactly as supplied\n    52\t     */\n    53\t    public static String email(char[] raw) throws ValidationException {\n    54\t        String value = text(raw, \"email\");\n    55\t        if (value.length() > EMAIL_MAX) {\n    56\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email is too long\");\n    57\t        }\n    58\t        int at = value.indexOf('@');\n    59\t        if (at < 0 || at != value.lastIndexOf('@')) {\n    60\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email must contain exactly one @\");\n    61\t        }\n    62\t        String local = value.substring(0, at);\n    63\t        String domain = value.substring(at + 1);\n    64\t        if (local.isEmpty() || local.length() > EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX) {\n    65\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email local part must be 1 to \" + EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX + \" characters\");\n    66\t        }\n    67\t        if (local.startsWith(\".\") || local.endsWith(\".\") || local.contains(\"..\")) {\n    68\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email local part has an invalid dot placement\");\n    69\t        }\n    70\t        for (int i = 0; i < local.length(); i++) {\n    71\t            if (LOCAL_PART.indexOf(local.charAt(i)) < 0) {\n    72\t                throw new ValidationException(\"email local part contains an unsupported character\");\n    73\t            }\n    74\t        }\n    75\t        if (domain.startsWith(\"[\") || domain.endsWith(\"]\")) {\n    76\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email domain must be a hostname, not an address literal\");\n    77\t        }\n    78\t        String[] labels = domain.split(\"\\\\.\", -1);\n    79\t        if (labels.length < 2) {\n    80\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email domain must contain at least two labels\");\n    81\t        }\n    82\t        for (String label : labels) {\n    83\t            if (label.isEmpty() || label.length() > EMAIL_LABEL_MAX) {\n    84\t                throw new ValidationException(\"email domain label length is out of range\");\n    85\t            }\n    86\t            if (label.startsWith(\"-\") || label.endsWith(\"-\")) {\n    87\t                throw new ValidationException(\"email domain label may not start or end with a hyphen\");\n    88\t            }\n    89\t            for (int i = 0; i < label.length(); i++) {\n    90\t                if (DOMAIN_LABEL.indexOf(label.charAt(i)) < 0) {\n    91\t                    throw new ValidationException(\"email domain label contains an unsupported character\");\n    92\t                }\n    93\t            }\n    94\t        }\n    95\t        String last = labels[labels.length - 1];\n    96\t        boolean allDigits = true;\n    97\t        for (int i = 0; i < last.length(); i++) {\n    98\t            if (!Character.isDigit(last.charAt(i))) {\n    99\t                allDigits = false;\n   100\t                break;\n   101\t            }\n   102\t        }\n   103\t        if (allDigits) {\n   104\t            throw new ValidationException(\"email domain top-level label may not be numeric\");\n   105\t        }\n   106\t        return value.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);\n   107\t    }\n   108\t\n   109\t    /**\n   110\t     * Usernames are stored and compared exactly as accepted; the character class below makes\n   111\t     * the stored form already canonical (lower-case ASCII, no separators other than '-').\n   112\t     */\n   113\t    public static String username(char[] raw) throws ValidationException {\n   114\t        String value = text(raw, \"username\");\n   115\t        if (value.length() < USERNAME_MIN || value.length() > USERNAME_MAX) {\n   116\t            throw new ValidationException(\"username must be \" + USERNAME_MIN + \" to \" + USERNAME_MAX + \" characters\");\n   117\t        }\n   118\t        char first = value.charAt(0);\n   119\t        if (!isAlpha(first)) {\n   120\t            throw new ValidationException(\"username must start with a letter\");\n   121\t        }\n   122\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n   123\t            char c = value.charAt(i);\n   124\t            if (USERNAME.indexOf(c) < 0) {\n   125\t                throw new ValidationException(\"username may contain only lower-case letters, digits and hyphens\");\n   126\t            }\n   127\t        }\n   128\t        if (value.endsWith(\"-\")) {\n   129\t            throw new ValidationException(\"username may not end with a hyphen\");\n   130\t        }\n   131\t        if (RESERVED_USERNAMES.contains(value)) {\n   132\t            throw new ValidationException(\"username is reserved\");\n   133\t        }\n   134\t        return value;\n   135\t    }\n   136\t\n   137\t    /**\n   138\t     * Passwords are never normalised: no trimming, no case folding, no Unicode normalisation.\n   139\t     * Rejection of leading/trailing whitespace and of control characters is what prevents an\n   140\t     * invisible suffix from silently becoming part of a different credential.\n   141\t     */\n   142\t    public static void password(char[] raw) throws ValidationException {\n   143\t        if (raw == null || raw.length == 0) {\n   144\t            throw new ValidationException(\"password is required\");\n   145\t        }\n   146\t        if (raw.length > PASSWORD_MAX) {\n   147\t            throw new ValidationException(\"password is too long\");\n   148\t        }\n   149\t        if (raw.length < PASSWORD_MIN) {\n   150\t            throw new ValidationException(\"password is too short\");\n   151\t        }\n   152\t        if (Character.isWhitespace(raw[0]) || Character.isWhitespace(raw[raw.length - 1])) {\n   153\t            throw new ValidationException(\"password may not start or end with whitespace\");\n   154\t        }\n   155\t        int letters = 0;\n   156\t        int digits = 0;\n   157\t        for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {\n   158\t            char c = raw[i];\n   159\t            if (Character.isISOControl(c) || c == '\\u007f' || Character.isSpaceChar(c)) {\n   160\t                throw new ValidationException(\"password may not contain control or invisible whitespace characters\");\n   161\t            }\n   162\t            if (Character.isSurrogate(c)) {\n   163\t                if (!Character.isHighSurrogate(c) || i + 1 >= raw.length || !Character.isLowSurrogate(raw[i + 1])) {\n   164\t                    throw new ValidationException(\"password contains an unpaired surrogate character\");\n   165\t                }\n   166\t                i++;\n   167\t            }\n   168\t            if (Character.isLetter(c)) {\n   169\t                letters++;\n   170\t            } else if (Character.isDigit(c)) {\n   171\t                digits++;\n   172\t            }\n   173\t        }\n   174\t        if (letters == 0 || digits == 0) {\n   175\t            throw new ValidationException(\"password must contain at least one letter and one digit\");\n   176\t        }\n   177\t    }\n   178\t\n   179\t    /**\n   180\t     * Parses a sign-in identifier. An identifier is <em>either</em> a complete email or a\n   181\t     * complete username - never a fuzzy blend - and the branch is chosen only by the presence of\n   182\t     * the one character that cannot appear in a username.\n   183\t     *\n   184\t     * <p>Only an email is case-folded, and that folding is the documented mailbox-equivalence\n   185\t     * rule. A username is matched exactly as validated, so no caller can reach an account by\n   186\t     * changing its letter case: {@link #username(char[])} already refuses anything but lower-case\n   187\t     * ASCII, which makes the stored form canonical.\n   188\t     */\n   189\t    public record Identity(boolean byEmail, String value) {\n   190\t    }\n   191\t\n   192\t    public static Identity identifier(char[] raw) throws ValidationException {\n   193\t        String value = text(raw, \"identifier\");\n   194\t        if (value.indexOf('@') >= 0) {\n   195\t            return new Identity(true, email(raw));\n   196\t        }\n   197\t        return new Identity(false, username(raw));\n   198\t    }\n   199\t\n   200\t    /**\n   201\t     * Rejects the password when it is built from the very identifier being registered.\n   202\t     * Scans the {@code char[]} directly instead of building a String so the password is not\n   203\t     * copied onto the heap where it could not be erased.\n   204\t     */\n   205\t    public static void passwordNotRelatedTo(char[] password, String... identifiers) throws ValidationException {\n   206\t        for (String id : identifiers) {\n   207\t            String needle = id;\n   208\t            int at = needle.indexOf('@');\n   209\t            if (at > 0) {\n   210\t                needle = needle.substring(0, at);\n   211\t            }\n   212\t            if (needle.length() >= 3 && containsIgnoreCase(password, needle)) {\n   213\t                throw new ValidationException(\"password must not contain the account name\");\n   214\t            }\n   215\t        }\n   216\t    }\n   217\t\n   218\t    private static boolean containsIgnoreCase(char[] haystack, String needle) {\n   219\t        int limit = haystack.length - needle.length();\n   220\t        for (int i = 0; i <= limit; i++) {\n   221\t            int j = 0;\n   222\t            while (j < needle.length()\n   223\t                && Character.toLowerCase(haystack[i + j]) == Character.toLowerCase(needle.charAt(j))) {\n   224\t                j++;\n   225\t            }\n   226\t            if (j == needle.length()) {\n   227\t                return true;\n   228\t            }\n   229\t        }\n   230\t        return false;\n   231\t    }\n   232\t\n   233\t    /** Shared structural checks for every free-text field. Returns the value with no change. */\n   234\t    private static String text(char[] raw, String field) throws ValidationException {\n   235\t        if (raw == null || raw.length == 0) {\n   236\t            throw new ValidationException(field + \" is required\");\n   237\t        }\n   238\t        if (raw.length > MAX_FIELD_CHARS) {\n   239\t            throw new ValidationException(field + \" is too long\");\n   240\t        }\n   241\t        for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {\n   242\t            char c = raw[i];\n   243\t            if (Character.isISOControl(c) || c == '\\u007f') {\n   244\t                throw new ValidationException(field + \" may not contain control characters\");\n   245\t            }\n   246\t            if (Character.isSurrogate(c)\n   247\t                && !(Character.isHighSurrogate(c) && i + 1 < raw.length && Character.isLowSurrogate(raw[i + 1]))) {\n   248\t                throw new ValidationException(field + \" contains an unpaired surrogate character\");\n   249\t            }\n   250\t        }\n   251\t        if (Character.isWhitespace(raw[0]) || Character.isWhitespace(raw[raw.length - 1])) {\n   252\t            throw new ValidationException(field + \" may not start or end with whitespace\");\n   253\t        }\n   254\t        for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {\n   255\t            if (!Character.isWhitespace(raw[i])) {\n   256\t                return new String(raw);\n   257\t            }\n   258\t        }\n   259\t        throw new ValidationException(field + \" must not be blank\");\n   260\t    }\n   261\t\n   262\t    private static boolean isAlpha(char c) {\n   263\t        return c >= 'a' && c <= 'z';\n   264\t    }\n   265\t\n   266\t    private static final String LOWER = \"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz\";\n   267\t    private static final String DIGITS = \"0123456789\";\n   268\t\n   269\t    private static final String LOCAL_PART = LOWER + \"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ\" + DIGITS + \"._%+-\";\n   270\t    private static final String DOMAIN_LABEL = LOWER + \"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ\" + DIGITS + \"-\";\n   271\t    private static final String USERNAME = LOWER + DIGITS + \"-\";\n   272\t}\n===== Main.java =====\n     1\tpackage app;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.io.Console;\n     4\timport java.io.IOException;\n     5\timport java.io.InputStream;\n     6\timport java.io.PrintStream;\n     7\timport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\n     8\timport java.sql.SQLException;\n     9\timport java.time.Clock;\n    10\timport java.util.Arrays;\n    11\timport java.util.function.Function;\n    12\t\n    13\t/**\n    14\t * Command line for registration, sign-in, session validation and sign-out.\n    15\t *\n    16\t * <p>Input handling:\n    17\t * <ul>\n    18\t *   <li>A password is read with {@code Console.readPassword()} whenever a terminal is attached, so\n    19\t *       it is never echoed. Only when standard input is redirected is a line read from it; even\n    20\t *       then nothing is echoed and the value is never written anywhere except into the hash.</li>\n    21\t *   <li>Every line read is length-capped <em>while reading</em>, so an oversized line cannot be\n    22\t *       buffered in full and rejected afterwards.</li>\n    23\t *   <li>Every buffer that held a credential is erased in a {@code finally} block. Erasure is\n    24\t *       best effort - the JVM copies {@code char[]} and {@code String} values in ways this class\n    25\t *       cannot control - and the README says so instead of overclaiming.</li>\n    26\t *   <li>Nothing the user typed is echoed back or written to the trail. What is printed is a fixed\n    27\t *       sentence plus, on a successful sign-in, the session cookie: the only way the person who\n    28\t *       just proved the password can use the session. On that path they already know the password,\n    29\t *       so the cookie is not a disclosure to a third party; it is never logged.</li>\n    30\t * </ul>\n    31\t *\n    32\t * <p>No credential is ever accepted as a command-line argument, so none can appear in a process\n    33\t * listing. That rule includes a session cookie: it is a bearer credential, valid to whoever holds\n    34\t * it, so {@code verify} and {@code logout} prompt for it on standard input and refuse a leftover\n    35\t * operand rather than using it. A prompted cookie is erased after use and reduced to a salted tag\n    36\t * in the trail.\n    37\t */\n    38\tpublic final class Main {\n    39\t\n    40\t    /** Stable exit codes so a caller can branch without parsing prose. */\n    41\t    static final int OK = 0;\n    42\t    static final int REFUSED = 2;\n    43\t    static final int BAD_CONFIG = 3;\n    44\t    static final int UNAVAILABLE = 4;\n    45\t    static final int BAD_USAGE = 64;\n    46\t\n    47\t    private static final String USAGE = \"\"\"\n    48\t        Usage:\n    49\t          register               create an account (email, username, password are prompted)\n    50\t          login                  sign in and print the session cookie\n    51\t          verify                 report whether a session cookie is currently valid (prompted)\n    52\t          logout                 revoke a session cookie (prompted)\n    53\t          revoke-sessions        revoke every live session of the named account\n    54\t\n    55\t        The session cookie for verify and logout is prompted for, never taken as an argument: an\n    56\t        argument is visible in other users' process listings, and a cookie is a bearer credential.\n    57\t\n    58\t        Environment:\n    59\t          APP_DATA_DIR           directory for the account store and audit log (required)\n    60\t          APP_DB_PASSWORD        database password, or APP_DB_PASSWORD_FILE naming a 0600 file\n    61\t          APP_ARGON2_MEMORY_KIB / APP_ARGON2_ITERATIONS / APP_ARGON2_PARALLELISM\n    62\t                                 optional; each is bounded to a range the store can also hold\n    63\t        \"\"\";\n    64\t\n    65\t    public static void main(String[] argv) {\n    66\t        System.exit(run(argv, System.getenv()::get, System.in, System.out, System.err));\n    67\t    }\n    68\t\n    69\t    static int run(String[] argv, Function<String, String> env, InputStream in,\n    70\t        PrintStream out, PrintStream err) {\n    71\t        // Options may appear before or after the command word, so the command is the first\n    72\t        // argument that is not a --option. Everything after it that is not an option is positional.\n    73\t        for (String argument : argv) {\n    74\t            if (argument.equals(\"--help\") || argument.equals(\"-h\")) {\n    75\t                out.print(USAGE);\n    76\t                return OK;\n    77\t            }\n    78\t        }\n    79\t        String command = null;\n    80\t        java.util.List<String> positional = new java.util.ArrayList<>();\n    81\t        for (String argument : argv) {\n    82\t            if (argument.startsWith(\"--\")) {\n    83\t                continue;\n    84\t            }\n    85\t            if (command == null) {\n    86\t                command = argument;\n    87\t            } else {\n    88\t                positional.add(argument);\n    89\t            }\n    90\t        }\n    91\t        if (command == null) {\n    92\t            err.print(USAGE);\n    93\t            return BAD_USAGE;\n    94\t        }\n    95\t        String[] operands = positional.toArray(new String[0]);\n    96\t\n    97\t        Config config = null;\n    98\t        Db database = null;\n    99\t        try {\n   100\t            config = Config.fromEnvironment(env, argv);\n   101\t            database = config.openDatabase();\n   102\t            // Schema first, explicitly: the throttle and session tables must exist before any\n   103\t            // command can consume a budget or issue a session.\n   104\t            database.applySchema();\n   105\t\n   106\t            PasswordHasher hasher = new PasswordHasher(config.argon2MemoryKib(),\n   107\t                config.argon2Iterations(), config.argon2Parallelism());\n   108\t            AuditLog audit = new AuditLog(config.auditLogFile());\n   109\t            Gate gate = new Gate(audit);\n   110\t            Clock clock = Clock.systemUTC();\n   111\t            Throttle throttle = new Throttle(database, clock);\n   112\t            AccountStore accounts = new AccountStore(database, hasher, throttle, gate, clock);\n   113\t            SessionStore sessions = new SessionStore(database, gate, clock);\n   114\t            AuthService auth = new AuthService(accounts, throttle, sessions, hasher, gate, clock);\n   115\t            Maintenance maintenance = new Maintenance(sessions, throttle, gate);\n   116\t            Main main = new Main(new Reader(in, System.console(), out), out, err, gate);\n   117\t\n   118\t            // Housekeeping before the command runs: expired/revoked sessions and throttle state\n   119\t            // whose window has elapsed are deleted so the tables the feature fills stay bounded.\n   120\t            // A failed sweep is reported and does not deny the command.\n   121\t            maintenance.run();\n   122\t            return switch (command) {\n   123\t                case \"register\" -> main.register(accounts);\n   124\t                case \"login\" -> main.login(auth);\n   125\t                case \"verify\" -> main.verify(operands, auth, main.reader);\n   126\t                case \"logout\" -> main.logout(operands, auth, main.reader);\n   127\t                case \"revoke-sessions\" -> main.revokeSessions(accounts, sessions);\n   128\t                default -> {\n   129\t                    err.print(USAGE);\n   130\t                    yield BAD_USAGE;\n   131\t                }\n   132\t            };\n   133\t        } catch (Config.ConfigException badEnvironment) {\n   134\t            // Configuration messages name the setting and the rule, never the value.\n   135\t            err.println(\"Configuration error: \" + badEnvironment.getMessage());\n   136\t            err.print(USAGE);\n   137\t            return BAD_CONFIG;\n   138\t        } catch (Config.AppConfiguredException storeUnreachable) {\n   139\t            err.println(AuthService.INTERNAL_MESSAGE);\n   140\t            return UNAVAILABLE;\n   141\t        } catch (SQLException | java.security.GeneralSecurityException storeFault) {\n   142\t            // The cause stays here, on the operator's own terminal, and never in a file that\n   143\t            // might outlive the process world-readable. The user-facing outcome is generic.\n   144\t            err.println(AuthService.INTERNAL_MESSAGE);\n   145\t            return UNAVAILABLE;\n   146\t        } catch (AuditLog.AuditException auditUnwritable) {\n   147\t            err.println(AuthService.INTERNAL_MESSAGE);\n   148\t            return UNAVAILABLE;\n   149\t        } catch (IOException inputClosed) {\n   150\t            err.println(\"The input ended before the command finished.\");\n   151\t            return UNAVAILABLE;\n   152\t        } catch (RuntimeException crash) {\n   153\t            // An unexpected defect - ours or a dependency's - must not print a stack trace where the\n   154\t            // user reads. A trace names absolute paths, the dependency layout and, through its cause\n   155\t            // chain, whatever a message happened to interpolate. The operator gets a generic line\n   156\t            // plus the exception's one-line identity: enough to look up in the source, not enough to\n   157\t            // fingerprint the deployment from the outside. Access is refused, not allowed.\n   158\t            err.println(AuthService.INTERNAL_MESSAGE);\n   159\t            err.println(\"NOTE unexpected failure of type \" + crash.getClass().getName());\n   160\t            return UNAVAILABLE;\n   161\t        } finally {\n   162\t            if (database != null) {\n   163\t                database.close();\n   164\t            }\n   165\t            if (config != null) {\n   166\t                config.close();\n   167\t            }\n   168\t        }\n   169\t    }\n   170\t\n   171\t    private final Reader reader;\n   172\t    private final 177\t        this.reader = reader;\n   178\t        this.out = out;\n   179\t        this.err = err;\n   180\t        this.gate = gate;\n   181\t    }\n   182\t\n   183\t    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ commands\n   184\t\n   185\t    private int register(AccountStore accounts)\n   186\t        throws IOException, SQLException, AuditLog.AuditException {\n   187\t        char[] emailChars;\n   188\t        char[] usernameChars;\n   189\t        char[] passwordChars;\n   190\t        try {\n   191\t            emailChars = reader.readLine(\"Email: \");\n   192\t            usernameChars = reader.readLine(\"Username: \");\n   193\t            passwordChars = reader.readSecret(\"Password: \");\n   194\t        } catch (InputTooLong oversized) {\n   195\t            out.println(REFUSED_MESSAGE);\n   196\t            recordQuietly(AuditLog.Event.REGISTRATION_REFUSED_INVALID_INPUT, \"input line too long\");\n   197\t            return REFUSED;\n   198\t        }\n   199\t        try {\n   200\t            String email;\n   201\t            String username;\n   202\t            try {\n   203\t                email = InputPolicy.email(emailChars);\n   204\t                username = InputPolicy.username(usernameChars);\n   205\t                InputPolicy.password(passwordChars);\n   206\t                InputPolicy.passwordNotRelatedTo(passwordChars, username, email);\n   207\t            } catch (InputPolicy.ValidationException invalid) {\n   208\t                // The rule is printed to the person attempting their own registration - a\n   209\t                // legitimate feedback channel - and recorded as a rule name, never a value.\n   210\t                out.println(\"Registration refused: outcome = accounts.register(passwordChars, email, username, sourceId);\n   219\t            } catch (AuditLog.AuditException auditUnwritable) {\n   220\t                // Every registration record is
